dar qoamyal qoshal char chto ainor yky dy aiinal qochol
chal oaral chor qo ordal oolty ochko ky ychty ainol ol o
cholchol qoshol
por cholal al aiiso aiiral alcfhar oshor oaiinar qoeear
tyainol chky qoshol ol chty oshal oeeto qoshal y chtal o
shalchky al chochy y chol oeeol chol yky qoshal al chtam
qodol aiinol sharol ochky al cfhar oroain or qo cholol y
chalol koychor
kol chol chol sheeto pypy amaiin oamom chor ydor chorar
charaiin cholchol o y cholal o qoamar cholchol ol qosham
y qochal yainol ol choar yain oear yamor o aldal y alal
yal qoamor aldy
par ar chochpy opcho qoamor aralartal al shkam qooror ar
alshkam ar alartal kchy shkar arky ar qotcho alshkam ky
shtar al shkam alartor chyshkar ar ky arar o tcho ar ky
chy shtol yal alaltol aleekal tcho cho tcho y kamartor o
taral tolyal shtom
kshol ol sochool chyain aleekam aryorol choal shal dain
oloal yal ar ky alalkol amar tor qokcho kcho oshal shal
chol oshol shkam olol ol al eekal chy ol yal char yshal
oshtom arar shkol oshtom amar choshkar chol amolty ochy
qoteeo chal y chtom arartal shkol ol choshtar ar chy oam
tom sholol ol yam shtal amar tor olyor o chochko alshtag
qokeey tar sholor tomartal amal
kair shor or shol olar ar ky aiinal chpy chochy ky shy
ochol ol amaltal aleekal al qo aiinal chyshkar cholchol
shololar shol cho alaltar cho choar koltom alaltol ekam
oeeal qo chal shar ar ko arar cham
pychor qokeeo amairol arsholor ar chaiin opshol oshpol
tom oshor shtar chaiin y keey oshtal ocho kcho cho shtag
alshkam olyol char chom chol chaiin alekol shtol y al o
orshol amar ol tom char amol ty chal aryoral qochy qokey
shkol char ochom
tar olpal choor kchol chochy tolom chaiin amolamom shol
char sharar shkom ychal omar tal chal oshy chkor o amor
olol shol tar ar chochy y tomolyal chain oshar oshalal
oshkol olarar ar kom oltal shol char qoeeal al ocho oeom
ochar okshol chyshtar shy
py yshfol amor chol pchy aiinal arol ar shar aror y kom
ycho shkor kolchar ar choshkol qoshy ochal amol amom o
ysho oeeo eeo
tychal qoeeo kshal shpal oeey omaral qosho aiin dainar

shar qokchy sholchtal ockhor yeear qokeeo keey qoshy shy
qochy qochol okchy qochor ockhal qoeear shal shtal shy
shyshtal qochar
kam shal qochol ol qoeeol aiin dan an shkor or ochtor om
qokeey ycthal al shkol yckhor qoeeo eey qoear talshy shy
arol shal yckhol year ar oshy char qochor oey shkan sho
octhar chol shy arqochy aiinol ol ockhy qoshy qoeear nam
ockhar shar yeol otchar kchy ockhor qochal ychal al oshy
omor qoshol qoeear ychkor sho talshy al ychar yckhor or
oey char qoeeol chor
tol ochy shor olshom ol aly al saiin ol alor ar alory an
qockhy aroshy oeol koly shal shtal yckhar qokchy qochor
shaly okchy aiinar ockhar aiinor shtol alo chal ochar ey
qoeeol qochor aiinarar ychor ykchol ockhor oshy kchy shy
charol ckhy ychom
ochy ain olor shal aiiinol ainal ysho oshor o aralalkal
cho char shtol shyar aiinar ainal ockhor qoeeear shkar
chol daiin char ar qochol oshar ckho char alochal kcho
shtolal chor charar shol oeey eey shalshtal ochol ochar
ycthol qotcheo qoshor or alshal aiinal ychor chor yshtam
aral shyal yeeol qochar ckho otckhor keeo ychkam
kchor ochal chor chorol oshy tairar ochar yshy ar ochar
chol charal ckhockhy qoeor cho yshor ochol kshol ol oshy
aiinal tcheo ol ckhar shyar yshom ycthor shtol ochal
chol shtam analory qoeear ckho octhor or shol kam ochy
cho yckhar sho shkol ol oshy yeeol shychy shkol sholol
shkor shtol shy
paiin korshkol oshpor aiinal o arar saiin ainor or shol
shar orshol or or shkol san aiinal aiinor arol aiinor an
shtol arar kolan orshkol an aiinor an aiinol or shol nom
dan an orshar om
kchal shkor chor karal ar chy shar shol daiin aiin osham
dar orshkol or shar qosham cho shal aiinol tolarar char
orshol aiinor shtar qocham odaiin qosham shal anorshar
ydaiin or shol chal sharal qosham sham qoshar ocham cham
odar dain qochal oshol anorshar oror shag
panam dy folkar chkar oeear orshar oeo daiin aiinol kom
chalchkal yshal ainal ain ysham aiinor or ocham olalar
otan dan or shol oshar qoeear air daiin sholshol ydar an
chkaiin qodar chal al anar qosham oshor choshal daiin
shtal shal al qochal sham ain ochar osham shor or ochy
ochar oshal dain dair qocham chor ol kar ainal al aiinam
ar karoeear shar qochal qoctham ydaiin sol sharal daiin
or shol odol chom
kaiinor chalchtal opy polol chy airalar shar yshol chal
oeeol dain ychar aiinoror or chol ol ar ar ar ocham anom

chalal daiin aim
painar shtol porol tshy ainar chochor chpar or oshar pan

oshomal dair aiin oshar shol ol
tolor or airam orshar yshar shal qockhol chochfy alamkam
orshol amkam oloror or shar oloror akam qochol ky amkam
orshal orshar ar orshar kam shol amkal ol oror shy akam
orshor or shol ol oror sho or shar amam o

shor daiin shom
kainor kshar qoor aisal chol ol dain pshy arockhy araram
oshom orshy oloral shal ory aiiror orchol ororor or sham
amkor or dain chor amkor qoal amam oryor shal chor dal
amko y daiir amkararor ory or chal choror or shar oshy
amkar shor shamor shor akam kam orshol shar ar y kararam
ainor ysho shor daiin chal orol amam orshol orar atam

pcho char dais choly sain ain ty charo chor shkal y pam
ain ocho yshkal al o cholain o o saiin otaro an daischom
y aiin oshy kal kal alalk daischom sain aiischom iischog
o osho air aischom shal chol ain oshyal sain qotaro y y
shor an dair aiin qo ochol aiin aischom y o dain qotary
oshal ain qocho shoran shal air daisshom qo odair dair
ar an oshy shol olaiin ochol daisshor dain saiin aischog
shor or qotoly o
panom oshy pol sar qo sain ochol aiilchar qoshal sair om
oshor aisshor toly cholaiil aisshom ar y shol shal al an
ochol aiischal shoror shor y aiin qotary ar ar daiiir
y aischo ain shalal y dain ain ochor aiilshal al dan air
sain qoshy qoshal tary ar cho y ochol aisshol ainar qo
chol aiil kal aiischog iischog ar shal
fain oran pain omom shalchol aischar shalchar ar apy kam

qo oshal dain alal air o olar tary yol toly koly chor ar

ainoran kory an
